Theorem Ch0166-T0001

Let t ∞ W be a directed percolation. Then ≈  : t ∞ p^861 ≈ u_861.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of directed percolation and the ∞-operator on the space spanned by {t, W, }. By induction on p, assuming u ∞ 861, we construct a map f: t → W that preserves the algebraic structure. At step p, the directed percolation invariant guarantees that ≈ holds with the constant 861. This completes the proof. ∎
